function swap(int* x, int* y) : void
    local t : int (4)
    t1 = *x                              ; int
    t = t1                               ; int
    t2 = *y                              ; int
    *x = t2                              ; int
    *y = t                               ; int
    return
endfunction

function set_ptr(int** pp, int* target) : void

    return
endfunction
